# Graph library API index

Generated by `python3 meta/library_api.py --write`; checked with `--check`.

This index covers 162 project-enrolled public source modules and 4162 explicit declarations, plus 102 registered migration families. Source links provide the full types, guards and proofs. Local declarations and Let bindings are omitted. Generated constructors, fields, instances, notations and module aliases are documented by their source; this is not an exhaustive compiler symbol table.

A fidelity verdict applies only to an explicitly enrolled primitive. A family verdict below describes that family's canonical contract; it does not audit every declaration in the same module. Unclassified declarations remain unclassified. Principal lemmas are exactly the family registry's `api_theorems`; compatibility certificates are not public imports.

The listing describes the intended public source surface. Independent installation, corpus-free dependency checks and assumptions gates are separate release requirements; an index entry does not establish them. Useful application results are a separate release component and are not included in this public-module index.

## Canonical families and principal lemmas

The status column records the migration of legacy aliases. In particular, `deprecated` does not deprecate the canonical public API. Upstream endpoints have no repository source link.

| [Cycle.foundations.path_subgraphs.path_subgraph](../cycle-theory/theories/foundations/path_subgraphs.v#L45) | Definition | FAITHFUL: path_subgraph P: P is a nonempty, connected, acyclic edge set in which every vertex has at most two arc ends (a loop counts twice). | [Digraph.constructions.digraph_sum.digraph_sum](../digraph-theory/theories/constructions/digraph_sum.v#L24) | Definition | FAITHFUL: The disjoint union (sum) of two finite digraphs on D1 + D2: the arcs of D1 between left vertices, those of D2 between right vertices, no arc across in either direction; loops and asymmetric arcs kept, either summand may be empty, #\|V\| = #\|D1\| + #\|D2\|. Not a join (heroes.djoin, X122 x122_dijoin add left-to-right arcs). The simple-graph union is upstream sjoin. | — |

| [Digraph.foundations.degree_balance.indeg](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/degree_balance.v#L48) | Definition | FAITHFUL: indeg v: the number of in-neighbours #\|[set u \| u --> v]\|, the companion of outdeg; a loop counts once; swaps with outdeg on the converse digraph. | — |
| [Digraph.foundations.degree_balance.balanced](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/degree_balance.v#L51) | Definition | FAITHFUL: balanced D: every vertex has equal in- and out-degree; no connectivity, looplessness, inhabitance or tour premise; invariant under converse; holds on the empty, arcless and loop digraphs and on disconnected unions of balanced digraphs. | Canonical: degree-balance |

### Digraph.foundations.neighbourhoods

Source: [digraph-theory/theories/foundations/neighbourhoods.v](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/neighbourhoods.v).

| Declaration | Kind | Explicit primitive fidelity | Registry roles |
|---|---|---|---|
| [Digraph.foundations.neighbourhoods.Nout](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/neighbourhoods.v#L47) | Definition | FAITHFUL: Nout v: the out-neighbourhood [set w \| v --> w] of a vertex of a finite digraph; a loop makes v its own out-neighbour; no looplessness, connectivity or inhabited-carrier premise; outdeg v = #\|Nout v\| by conversion; the converse digraph swaps it with Nin. | Canonical: directed-neighbourhood |
| [Digraph.foundations.neighbourhoods.Nin](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/neighbourhoods.v#L48) | Definition | FAITHFUL: Nin v: the in-neighbourhood [set u \| u --> v]; a loop makes v its own in-neighbour; B16's indeg v = #\|Nin v\| by conversion; the converse digraph swaps it with Nout. | — |
| [Digraph.foundations.neighbourhoods.Nout2](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/neighbourhoods.v#L53) | Definition | FAITHFUL: Nout2 v: the second out-neighbourhood, the vertices w at directed distance exactly two: w != v, w is not an out-neighbour of v, and some out-neighbour u of v has u --> w. Both exclusions are kept even when a loop or a digon gives a walk of length two back to v or to an out-neighbour (Nout2_neq, Nout2_notin_Nout, digon_notin_Nout2). | — |

### Digraph.foundations.tournament_unavoidability

Source: [digraph-theory/theories/foundations/tournament_unavoidability.v](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/tournament_unavoidability.v).

|---|---|---|---|
| [Digraph.foundations.tournament_unavoidability.tournament_unavoidable](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/tournament_unavoidability.v#L28) | Definition | FAITHFUL: Exact unvd.unavoidable: every bundled tournament with exactly N vertices contains an arbitrary finite digraph D through an injective map preserving arcs one way (upstream is_dhom on to_GT); tournament_unavoidable_unbundledE proves it equivalent to the unbundled irreflexive, semicomplete and asymmetric reading via the same-carrier factory. Not induced; loops and digons are allowed in D and then never contained. | — |
| [Digraph.foundations.tournament_unavoidability.unavoidability_number](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/tournament_unavoidability.v#L33) | Definition | FAITHFUL: Exact unvd.unvd: N-unavoidable and not M-unavoidable for EVERY smaller natural M. The empty digraph has value 0, values are unique and at least #\|D\|; no existence or positivity guard. | — |
| [Digraph.foundations.tournament_unavoidability.linearly_unavoidable](../digraph-theory/theories/foundations/tournament_unavoidability.v#L36) | Definition | FAITHFUL: Exact X221.x221_linearly_unavoidable: one natural C before all digraphs and values, F D -> unavoidability_number D N -> N <= C * #\|D\|; members without a value impose no bound. | — |

| [Extremal.foundations.fractional_hadwiger.frac_clique_minor](../extremal-graph-theory/theories/foundations/fractional_hadwiger.v#L44) | Definition | FAITHFUL: frac_clique_minor B w r: a feasible point of the fractional clique-minor LP of G with value r: non-negative weights on an indexed family of branch sets, each NON-EMPTY and connected, pairwise adjacent for distinct indices, every vertex covered by total weight at most 1, r the total weight (branch sets need not be disjoint). ### GTBase.common

| Declaration | Kind | Explicit primitive fidelity | Registry roles |
|---|---|---|---|
| [GTBase.common.sg_edge_setE](../base/theories/common.v#L51) | Lemma | Unclassified | Principal lemma: simple-graph-edge-set |

| [GTBase.common.edge_count](../base/theories/common.v#L73) | Definition | FAITHFUL: The number of edges of a simple graph: the cardinality of the upstream edge set E(G) of unordered adjacent pairs. The adjacent ordered pairs taken in one orientation (enum_rank order) have the same count, unconditionally (edge_count_rank); both orientations give twice as much. K_0 and K_1 have no edge, K_2 one, K_3 three, K_n C(n,2) (edge_count_Kn), and the count is an isomorphism invariant (edge_count_diso). | Canonical: edge-count |
| [GTBase.common.edge_count_rank](../base/theories/common.v#L75) | Lemma | Unclassified | Principal lemma: edge-count |
| [GTBase.common.sg_edge_set_cliqueE](../base/theories/common.v#L101) | Lemma | Unclassified | Principal lemma: simple-graph-edge-set |
| [GTBase.common.edge_disjoint](../base/theories/common.v#L119) | Definition | Unclassified | — |
| [GTBase.common.edges_between](../base/theories/common.v#L138) | Definition | FAITHFUL: The number of ORDERED pairs (a, b) with a in A, b in B and a -- b, for arbitrary, possibly overlapping A and B: an edge inside A :&: B is counted in both orientations. Symmetric in A and B; with the non-edge count it partitions A x B; on disjoint sets it is the number of edges of E(G) meeting both sets (edges_between_cross). K_n: \|A\|\|B\| - \|A :&: B\|; K_2 taken whole: 2. | Canonical: edges-between |
| [GTBase.common.nonedges_between](../base/theories/common.v#L141) | Definition | FAITHFUL: The number of ORDERED pairs (a, b) with a in A, b in B and ~~ (a -- b), including the diagonal pair (x, x) of each x in A :&: B; on disjoint sets it agrees with the number of complement edges between them (nonedges_between_compl); with overlap that reading can fail (nonedges_between_compl_overlap). K_n: \|A :&: B\|. | — |
| [GTBase.common.cut_size](../base/theories/common.v#L155) | Definition | FAITHFUL: The number of edges of E(G) with one end in A and the other outside; zero for the empty and the full side, invariant under complementing the side, equal to the ordered edge count between A and its complement and to the number of non-monochromatic edges under membership in A. | Canonical: cut-size |
| [GTBase.common.perfect_matching](../base/theories/common.v#L181) | Definition | FAITHFUL: A matching (members are edges, two members through a vertex are equal) whose members cover every vertex; equivalently every member is an edge and every vertex lies in exactly one member (perfect_matching_exactly_oneP). On the empty graph set0 is a perfect matching; a family with a non-edge member never is; the members pair the vertices up, so a graph of odd order has none. | Canonical: perfect-matching |

### GTBase.edge_colourings

Source: [base/theories/edge_colourings.v](../base/theories/edge_colourings.v).

| Declaration | Kind | Explicit primitive fidelity | Registry roles |
|---|---|---|---|
| [GTBase.edge_colourings.proper_edge_colouring](../base/theories/edge_colourings.v#L51) | Definition | FAITHFUL: Supplied set map {set G} -> C over an eqType palette, read on the doubletons of actual edges: two distinct edges at a common endpoint get different colours (endpoint form), equivalent to the two corpus edge-pair forms (nonempty intersection / not disjoint). Values off E(G) are irrelevant; labels are kept, injective relabelling preserves and reflects properness; the domain is inhabited for every graph, so no map into an empty palette exists. | Canonical: proper-edge-colouring |
| [GTBase.edge_colourings.proper_pair_edge_colouring](../base/theories/edge_colourings.v#L54) | Definition | FAITHFUL: Supplied ordered-pair map G -> G -> C over an eqType palette, symmetric on ALL pairs (adjacent or not), with different colours on two distinct neighbours of a vertex. | [GTBase.incidence.incidence_degree](../base/theories/incidence.v#L27) | Definition | FAITHFUL: The number of members of an arbitrary finite family F : {set {set T}} containing v, on any finType; no edge, uniformity or nonemptiness premise. The empty family and empty members count nothing, a singleton member counts once at its vertex, the count is at most #\|F\|, monotone in F and subtractive on subfamilies; restricted to the members inside W it is zero outside W and the full count for W = setT. At E(G) it is #\|N(v)\| (GTBase.common.incidence_degree_edges). | Canonical: incidence-degree |

| [GTBase.walks_paths.seq_consecutive](../base/theories/walks_paths.v#L466) | Definition | FAITHFUL: seq_consecutive s u v holds when u and v are adjacent entries of the raw sequence s, in either order: (u, v) or (v, u) occurs in zip s (behead s), equivalently [:: u; v] or [:: v; u] occurs contiguously in s. Prop codomain, symmetric, with no edge, path, uniqueness or distinct-endpoint premise: [::] and one-entry sequences relate nothing, [:: x; y] relates exactly x and y, a repeated adjacent entry relates a value to itself, the closing pair of a cyclic sequence is not consecutive unless also adjacent, and it is invariant under rev; consecutive entries of a MathComp path or of a packaged coq-graph-theory Path are related by its relation. | Canonical: consecutive-in-path |

| [GTBase.walks_paths.seq_cyclic_consecutiveb](../base/theories/walks_paths.v#L669) | Definition | FAITHFUL: seq_cyclic_consecutiveb c u v holds when u and v are adjacent in the cyclic order of the raw sequence c, in either order: (u, v) or (v, u) occurs in zip c (rot 1 c), i.e. among the adjacent pairs and the closing pair (last, first). No path, edge, uniqueness, size or distinctness premise: [::] relates nothing, [:: a] relates a to itself, [:: a; b] relates exactly a and b, the closing pair of a longer sequence is adjacent, a repeated value may be self-adjacent through the closing pair; symmetric and invariant under rot, rotr and rev; on a MathComp cycle cyclically consecutive entries are related. | Canonical: consecutive-in-cycle |

| [GTBase.walks_paths.has_ucycle_length](../base/theories/walks_paths.v#L2402) | Definition | FAITHFUL: has_ucycle_length r n: some MathComp ucycle of r has exactly n entries; RAW, no length guard: 0 always (empty ucycle), 1 exactly with a loop, 2 exactly with a two-way distinct pair (an edge on a simple graph); agrees with has_cycle_length only under 2 < n. | Canonical: cycle-lengths |
| [GTBase.walks_paths.has_cycle_length](../base/theories/walks_paths.v#L2405) | Definition | FAITHFUL: has_cycle_length r n: some genuine cycle (seq_cycle, more than two entries) has exactly n entries; impossible for n <= 2. | — |
| [GTBase.walks_paths.no_ucycle_length_between](../base/theories/walks_paths.v#L2408) | Definition | FAITHFUL: no_ucycle_length_between r lo hi: no ucycle has between lo and hi entries, inclusive; RAW: an interval containing 0 always fails, one containing 2 excludes two-way pairs, reversed intervals are vacuous; agrees with the genuine exclusion only under 2 < lo. | — |
| [GTBase.walks_paths.no_cycle_length_between](../base/theories/walks_paths.v#L2412) | Definition | FAITHFUL: no_cycle_length_between r lo hi: no ucycle with more than two entries has between lo and hi entries, inclusive; dual to has_cycle_length over the interval; always holds for hi <= 2. | — |
| [GTBase.walks_paths.no_cycle_length](../base/theories/walks_paths.v#L2416) | Definition | FAITHFUL: no_cycle_length r n: every ucycle with more than two entries has size != n (Boolean disequality); the genuine interval [n, n], and the negation of has_cycle_length r n. | — |

### Reconstruction.foundations.decks

Source: [reconstruction-theory/theories/foundations/decks.v](../reconstruction-theory/theories/foundations/decks.v).

| Declaration | Kind | Explicit primitive fidelity | Registry roles |
|---|---|---|---|
| [Reconstruction.foundations.decks.vdel_card](../reconstruction-theory/theories/foundations/decks.v#L28) | Definition | FAITHFUL: vdel_card v: the card G - v, the subgraph induced on the vertices other than v; equal to upstream's induced [set~ v] (vdel_cardE) and of order #\|G\| - 1 (card_vdel_card). | — |
| [Reconstruction.foundations.decks.same_deck](../reconstruction-theory/theories/foundations/decks.v#L30) | Definition | FAITHFUL: same_deck G H: a bijection f of the vertex sets with vdel_card v isomorphic to vdel_card (f v) for every v; the multiset-of-cards reading (every card counted with multiplicity); forces equal orders (same_deck_card); no order guard (the conjecture's three-vertex guards live in the statement). | Canonical: deletion-decks |
| [Reconstruction.foundations.decks.reconstructible](../reconstruction-theory/theories/foundations/decks.v#L35) | Definition | FAITHFUL: reconstructible G: every H with the same deck is isomorphic to G; no guard. | — |
| [Reconstruction.foundations.decks.same_edge_deck](../reconstruction-theory/theories/foundations/decks.v#L38) | Definition | FAITHFUL: same_edge_deck G H: a bijection f of the edge index types {e \| e \in E(G)} with del_edge_set G [set e] isomorphic to del_edge_set H [set f e] for every edge; forces equal edge counts (same_edge_deck_card_edge); any two edgeless graphs have the same edge deck (edgeless_same_edge_deck), so a positive edge-count guard is needed and lives in the statements. | — |
| [Reconstruction.foundations.decks.edge_reconstructible](../reconstruction-theory/theories/foundations/decks.v#L44) | Definition | FAITHFUL: edge_reconstructible G: every H with the same edge deck is isomorphic to G; no guard. | — |
